bind-key    -T prefix       =                         choose-buffer -Z
bind-key    -T prefix       >                         display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{pane_index} (#{pane_id})" -x P -y P "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},Go To Top,}" < { send-keys -X history-top } "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},Go To Bottom,}" > { send-keys -X history-bottom } '' "#{?#{==:#{pane_mode},copy-mode},#{?copy_line_numbers,Hide Line Numbers,Show Line Numbers},}" L { send-keys -X line-numbers-toggle } "#{?#{==:#{pane_mode},copy-mode},#{?refresh_active,Refresh Off,Refresh On},}" r { send-keys -X refresh-toggle } '' "#{?#{&&:#{buffer_size},#{!:#{pane_in_mode}}},Paste #[underscore]#{=/9/...:buffer_sample},}" p { paste-buffer } '' "#{?mouse_word,Search For #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-r { if-shell -F "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},0,1}" "copy-mode -t=" ; send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_line,Copy Line,}" l { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_line}" } '' "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" C-h { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z }

send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_line,Copy Line,}" l { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_line}" } '' "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" C-h { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z } }

### list-N
$ list-keys-N
C-b Tab     Switch to a window
C-b Space   Select next layout
C-b !       Break pane to a new window
C-b "       Split window vertically
C-b #       List all paste buffers
C-b $       Rename current session
C-b %       Split window horizontally
C-b &       Kill current window
C-b '       Prompt for window index to select
C-b (       Switch to previous client
C-b )       Switch to next client
C-b *       New floating pane
C-b ,       Rename current window
C-b -       Delete the most recent paste buffer
C-b .       Move the current window
C-b /       Describe key binding
C-b 0       Select window 0
C-b 1       Select window 1
C-b 2       Select window 2
C-b 3       Select window 3
C-b 4       Select window 4
C-b 5       Select window 5
C-b 6       Select window 6
C-b 7       Select window 7
C-b 8       Select window 8
C-b 9       Select window 9
C-b :       Prompt for a command
C-b ;       Move to the previously active pane
C-b <       Display window menu
C-b =       Choose a paste buffer from a list
C-b >       Display pane menu
C-b ?       List key bindings
C-b @       Toggle pane between floating and tiled
C-b C       Customize options
C-b D       Choose and detach a client from a list
C-b E       Spread panes out evenly
C-b L       Switch to the last client
C-b M       Clear the marked pane
C-b T       Change the pane title
C-b [       Enter copy mode
C-b ]       Paste the most recent paste buffer
C-b c       Create a new window
C-b d       Detach the current client
C-b f       Search for a pane
C-b g       Move a floating pane
C-b i       Display window information
C-b l       Select the previously current window
C-b m       Toggle the marked pane
C-b n       Select the next window
C-b o       Select the next pane
C-b p       Select the previous window
C-b q       Display pane numbers
C-b r       Redraw the current client
C-b s       Choose a session from a list
C-b t       Show a clock
C-b w       Choose a window from a list
C-b x       Kill the active pane
C-b z       Zoom the active pane
C-b {       Swap the active pane with the pane above
C-b }       Swap the active pane with the pane below
C-b ~       Show messages
C-b DC      Reset so the visible part of the window follows the cursor
C-b PPage   Enter copy mode and scroll up
C-b BTab    Switch to a session
C-b Up      Select the pane above the active pane
C-b Down    Select the pane below the active pane
C-b Left    Select the pane to the left of the active pane
C-b Right   Select the pane to the right of the active pane
C-b M-1     Set the even-horizontal layout
C-b M-2     Set the even-vertical layout
C-b M-3     Set the main-horizontal layout
C-b M-4     Set the main-vertical layout
C-b M-5     Select the tiled layout
C-b M-6     Set the main-horizontal-mirrored layout
C-b M-7     Set the main-vertical-mirrored layout
C-b M-n     Select the next window with an alert
C-b M-o     Rotate through the panes in reverse
C-b M-p     Select the previous window with an alert
C-b M-Up    Resize the pane up by 5
C-b M-Down  Resize the pane down by 5
C-b M-Left  Resize the pane left by 5
C-b M-Right Resize the pane right by 5
C-b C-b     Send the prefix key
C-b C-o     Rotate through the panes
C-b C-z     Suspend the current client
C-b C-Up    Resize the pane up
C-b C-Down  Resize the pane down
C-b C-Left  Resize the pane left
C-b C-Right Resize the pane right
C-b S-Up    Move the visible part of the window up
C-b S-Down  Move the visible part of the window down
C-b S-Left  Move the visible part of the window left
C-b S-Right Move the visible part of the window right

C-b C-o                       Rotate through the panes
C-b C-z                       Suspend the current client
C-b C-Up                      Resize the pane up
C-b C-Down                    Resize the pane down
C-b C-Left                    Resize the pane left
C-b C-Right                   Resize the pane right
C-b S-Up                      Move the visible part of the window up
C-b S-Down                    Move the visible part of the window down
C-b S-Left                    Move the visible part of the window left
C-b S-Right                   Move the visible part of the window right
C-b MouseDown1Pane            select-pane -t = \; send-keys -M
C-b MouseDown1Status          switch-client -t =
C-b MouseDown1Border          select-pane -t =
C-b MouseDown1ScrollbarUp     if-shell -F -t = "#{pane_in_mode}" { send-keys -X page-up } { copy-mode -u }
C-b MouseDown1ScrollbarDown   if-shell -F -t = "#{pane_in_mode}" { send-keys -X page-down } { copy-mode -d }
C-b MouseDown1Control7        if-shell -F -t = "#{pane_floating_flag}" { join-pane } { break-pane -W }
C-b MouseDown1Control8        resize-pane -Z
C-b MouseDown1Control9        display-menu -O -T "Kill pane #{pane_index}?" -t = -x M -y M Yes y { kill-pane -t = } No n {  }
C-b MouseDown2Pane            select-pane -t = \; if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { paste-buffer -p }
C-b MouseDown3Pane            if-shell -F -t = "#{||:#{mouse_any_flag},#{&&:#{pane_in_mode},#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},0,1}}}" { select-pane -t = ; send-keys -M } { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{pane_index} (#{pane_id})" -t = -x M -y M "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},Go To Top,}" < { send-keys -X history-top } "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},Go To Bottom,}" > { send-keys -X history-bottom } '' "#{?#{==:#{pane_mode},copy-mode},#{?copy_line_numbers,Hide Line Numbers,Show Line Numbers},}" L { send-keys -X line-numbers-toggle } "#{?#{==:#{pane_mode},copy-mode},#{?refresh_active,Refresh Off,Refresh On},}" r { send-keys -X refresh-toggle } '' "#{?#{&&:#{buffer_size},#{!:#{pane_in_mode}}},Paste #[underscore]#{=/9/...:buffer_sample},}" p { paste-buffer } '' "#{?mouse_word,Search For #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-r { if-shell -F "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},0,1}" "copy-mode -t=" ; send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_line,Copy Line,}" l { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_line}" } '' "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" C-h { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z } }
C-b MouseDown3Status          display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{window_index}:#{window_name}" -t = -x W -y W "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Left" l { swap-window -t :-1 } "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Right" r { swap-window -t :+1 } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-window } '' Kill X { kill-window } Respawn R { respawn-window -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } Rename n { command-prompt -F -I "#W" { rename-window -t "#{window_id}" "%%" } } '' "New After" w { new-window -a } "New At End" W { new-window }
C-b MouseDown3StatusLeft      run-shell -C "display-menu -t= -xM -yW -T '#[align=centre]#{session_name}'  #{S/t:#{?#{&&:#{<:#{loop_index},6},#{!:#{session_active}}},'Switch To #[underscore]#{session_name}' '' {switch-client -t=#{session_id}#} ,}} '' 'Renumber' 'N' {move-window -r} 'Rename' 'r' {command-prompt -I '#S' {rename-session -- '%%'}} 'Detach' 'd' {detach-client} '' 'New Session' 's' {new-session} 'New Window' 'w' {new-window}"
C-b MouseDown3Empty           display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{window_index}:#{window_name}" -t = -x M -y M "New Pane" p { new-pane ; join-pane } "New Window" w { new-window }
C-b MouseDrag1Pane            if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -M }
C-b MouseDrag1Border          resize-pane -M
C-b MouseDrag1ScrollbarSlider if-shell -F -t = "#{pane_in_mode}" { send-keys -X scroll-to-mouse } { copy-mode -S }
C-b WheelDownStatus           next-window
C-b WheelUpPane               if-shell -F "#{||:#{alternate_on},#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -e }
C-b WheelUpStatus             previous-window
C-b DoubleClick1Pane          select-pane -t = \; if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -H ; send-keys -X select-word ; run-shell -d 0.3 ; send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel }
C-b TripleClick1Pane          select-pane -t = \; if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -H ; send-keys -X select-line ; run-shell -d 0.3 ; send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel }

send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; ### list-N-table
$ list-keys-N-Tprefix
C-b Tab     Switch to a window
C-b Space   Select next layout
C-b !       Break pane to a new window
C-b "       Split window vertically
C-b #       List all paste buffers
C-b $       Rename current session
C-b %       Split window horizontally
C-b &       Kill current window
C-b '       Prompt for window index to select
C-b (       Switch to previous client
C-b )       Switch to next client
C-b *       New floating pane
C-b ,       Rename current window
C-b -       Delete the most recent paste buffer
C-b .       Move the current window
C-b /       Describe key binding
C-b 0       Select window 0
C-b 1       Select window 1
C-b 2       Select window 2
C-b 3       Select window 3
C-b 4       Select window 4
C-b 5       Select window 5
C-b 6       Select window 6
C-b 7       Select window 7
C-b 8       Select window 8
C-b 9       Select window 9
C-b :       Prompt for a command
C-b ;       Move to the previously active pane
C-b <       Display window menu
C-b =       Choose a paste buffer from a list
C-b >       Display pane menu
C-b ?       List key bindings
C-b @       Toggle pane between floating and tiled
C-b C       Customize options
C-b D       Choose and detach a client from a list
C-b E       Spread panes out evenly
C-b L       Switch to the last client
C-b M       Clear the marked pane
C-b T       Change the pane title
C-b [       Enter copy mode
C-b ]       Paste the most recent paste buffer
C-b c       Create a new window
C-b d       Detach the current client
C-b f       Search for a pane
C-b g       Move a floating pane
C-b i       Display window information
C-b l       Select the previously current window
C-b m       Toggle the marked pane
C-b n       Select the next window
C-b o       Select the next pane
C-b p       Select the previous window
C-b q       Display pane numbers
C-b r       Redraw the current client
C-b s       Choose a session from a list
C-b t       Show a clock
C-b w       Choose a window from a list
C-b x       Kill the active pane
C-b z       Zoom the active pane
C-b {       Swap the active pane with the pane above
C-b }       Swap the active pane with the pane below
C-b ~       Show messages
C-b DC      Reset so the visible part of the window follows the cursor
C-b PPage   Enter copy mode and scroll up
C-b BTab    Switch to a session
C-b Up      Select the pane above the active pane
C-b Down    Select the pane below the active pane
C-b Left    Select the pane to the left of the active pane
C-b Right   Select the pane to the right of the active pane
C-b M-1     Set the even-horizontal layout
C-b M-2     Set the even-vertical layout
C-b M-3     Set the main-horizontal layout
C-b M-4     Set the main-vertical layout
C-b M-5     Select the tiled layout
C-b M-6     Set the main-horizontal-mirrored layout
C-b M-7     Set the main-vertical-mirrored layout
C-b M-n     Select the next window with an alert
C-b M-o     Rotate through the panes in reverse
C-b M-p     Select the previous window with an alert
C-b M-Up    Resize the pane up by 5
C-b M-Down  Resize the pane down by 5
C-b M-Left  Resize the pane left by 5
C-b M-Right Resize the pane right by 5
C-b C-b     Send the prefix key
C-b C-o     Rotate through the panes
C-b C-z     Suspend the current client
C-b C-Up    Resize the pane up
C-b C-Down  Resize the pane down
C-b C-Left  Resize the pane left
C-b C-Right Resize the pane right
C-b S-Up    Move the visible part of the window up
C-b S-Down  Move the visible part of the window down
C-b S-Left  Move the visible part of the window left
C-b S-Right Move the visible part of the window right

C-b MouseDown3StatusLeft      run-shell -C "display-menu -t= -xM -yW -T '#[align=centre]#{session_name}'  #{S/t:#{?#{&&:#{<:#{loop_index},6},#{!:#{session_active}}},'Switch To #[underscore]#{session_name}' '' {switch-client -t=#{session_id}#} ,}} '' 'Renumber' 'N' {move-window -r} 'Rename' 'r' {command-prompt -I '#S' {rename-session -- '%%'}} 'Detach' 'd' {detach-client} '' 'New Session' 's' {new-session} 'New Window' 'w' {new-window}"
C-b MouseDown3Empty           display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{window_index}:#{window_name}" -t = -x M -y M "New Pane" p { new-pane ; join-pane } "New Window" w { new-window }
C-b MouseDrag1Pane            if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -M }

C-b MouseDrag1ScrollbarSlider if-shell -F -t = "#{pane_in_mode}" { send-keys -X scroll-to-mouse } { copy-mode -S }
C-b WheelDownStatus           next-window
C-b WheelUpPane               if-shell -F "#{||:#{alternate_on},#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -e }

C-b DoubleClick1Pane          select-pane -t = \; if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -H ; send-keys -X select-word ; run-shell -d 0.3 ; send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel }
C-b TripleClick1Pane          select-pane -t = \; if-shell -F "#{||:#{pane_in_mode},#{mouse_any_flag}}" { send-keys -M } { copy-mode -H ; send-keys -X select-line ; run-shell -d 0.3 ; send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel }

send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_line,Copy Line,}" l { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_line}" } '' "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" C-h { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z }
C-b M-MouseDown3Status        display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{window_index}:#{window_name}" -t = -x W -y W "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Left" l { swap-window -t :-1 } "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Right" r { swap-window -t :+1 } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-window } '' Kill X { kill-window } Respawn R { respawn-window -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } Rename n { command-prompt -F -I "#W" { rename-window -t "#{window_id}" "%%" } } '' "New After" w { new-window -a } "New At End" W { new-window }

### list-T-prefix
$ list-keys-Tprefix
bind-key    -T prefix Tab     new-pane -E -X 0 -Y 0 -x "75%" -y "30%" \; move-pane -P bottom-centre \; switch-mode -kw
bind-key    -T prefix Space   next-layout
bind-key    -T prefix !       break-pane
bind-key    -T prefix \"      split-window
bind-key    -T prefix \#      list-buffers
bind-key    -T prefix \$      command-prompt -I "#S" { rename-session "%%" }
bind-key    -T prefix \%      split-window -h
bind-key    -T prefix &       confirm-before -p "kill-window #W? (y/n)" kill-window
bind-key    -T prefix \'      command-prompt -p index { select-window -t ":%%" }
bind-key    -T prefix (       switch-client -p
bind-key    -T prefix )       switch-client -n
bind-key    -T prefix *       new-pane
bind-key    -T prefix ,       command-prompt -I "#W" { rename-window "%%" }
bind-key    -T prefix -       delete-buffer
bind-key    -T prefix .       command-prompt { move-window -t "%%" }
bind-key    -T prefix /       command-prompt -k -p key { list-keys -1N "%%" }
bind-key    -T prefix 0       select-window -t :=0
bind-key    -T prefix 1       select-window -t :=1
bind-key    -T prefix 2       select-window -t :=2
bind-key    -T prefix 3       select-window -t :=3
bind-key    -T prefix 4       select-window -t :=4
bind-key    -T prefix 5       select-window -t :=5
bind-key    -T prefix 6       select-window -t :=6
bind-key    -T prefix 7       select-window -t :=7
bind-key    -T prefix 8       select-window -t :=8
bind-key    -T prefix 9       select-window -t :=9
bind-key    -T prefix :       command-prompt
bind-key    -T prefix \;      last-pane
bind-key    -T prefix <       display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{window_index}:#{window_name}" -x W -y W "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Left" l { swap-window -t :-1 } "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Right" r { swap-window -t :+1 } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-window } '' Kill X { kill-window } Respawn R { respawn-window -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } Rename n { command-prompt -F -I "#W" { rename-window -t "#{window_id}" "%%" } } '' "New After" w { new-window -a } "New At End" W { new-window }

send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z } }
bind-key  -T root MouseDown3Status          display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{window_index}:#{window_name}" -t = -x W -y W "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Left" l { swap-window -t :-1 } "#{?#{>:#{session_windows},1},,-}Swap Right" r { swap-window -t :+1 } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-window } '' Kill X { kill-window } Respawn R { respawn-window -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } Rename n { command-prompt -F -I "#W" { rename-window -t "#{window_id}" "%%" } } '' "New After" w { new-window -a } "New At End" W { new-window }
bind-key  -T root MouseDown3StatusLeft      run-shell -C "display-menu -t= -xM -yW -T '#[align=centre]#{session_name}'  #{S/t:#{?#{&&:#{<:#{loop_index},6},#{!:#{session_active}}},'Switch To #[underscore]#{session_name}' '' {switch-client -t=#{session_id}#} ,}} '' 'Renumber' 'N' {move-window -r} 'Rename' 'r' {command-prompt -I '#S' {rename-session -- '%%'}} 'Detach' 'd' {detach-client} '' 'New Session' 's' {new-session} 'New Window' 'w' {new-window}"

send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_line,Copy Line,}" l { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_line}" } '' "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" C-h { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z }

bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi r                 send-keys -X refresh-now
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi t                 command-prompt -1P -p "(jump to forward)" { send-keys -X jump-to-forward -- "%%" }
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi v                 send-keys -X rectangle-toggle
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi w                 send-keys -X next-word
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi z                 send-keys -X scroll-middle
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi \{                send-keys -X previous-paragraph
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi \}                send-keys -X next-paragraph
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi BSpace            send-keys -X cursor-left
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi Home              send-keys -X start-of-line
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi End               send-keys -X end-of-line
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi NPage             send-keys -X page-down
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi PPage             send-keys -X page-up
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi Up                send-keys -X cursor-up
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi Down              send-keys -X cursor-down
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi Left              send-keys -X cursor-left
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi Right             send-keys -X cursor-right
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi MouseDown1Pane    select-pane
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi MouseDrag1Pane    select-pane \; send-keys -X begin-selection
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi MouseDragEnd1Pane send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi WheelDownPane     select-pane \; send-keys -X -N 5 scroll-down
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi WheelUpPane       select-pane \; send-keys -X -N 5 scroll-up
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi DoubleClick1Pane  select-pane \; send-keys -X select-word \; run-shell -d 0.3 \; send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi TripleClick1Pane  select-pane \; send-keys -X select-line \; run-shell -d 0.3 \; send-keys -X copy-pipe-and-cancel
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi M-x               send-keys -X jump-to-mark
bind-key  -T copy-mode-vi C-[               send-keys -X clear-selection

### list-P
$ list-keys-N-PPFX
PFX Tab     Switch to a window
PFX Space   Select next layout
PFX !       Break pane to a new window
PFX "       Split window vertically
PFX #       List all paste buffers
PFX $       Rename current session
PFX %       Split window horizontally
PFX &       Kill current window
PFX '       Prompt for window index to select
PFX (       Switch to previous client
PFX )       Switch to next client
PFX *       New floating pane
PFX ,       Rename current window
PFX -       Delete the most recent paste buffer
PFX .       Move the current window
PFX /       Describe key binding
PFX 0       Select window 0
PFX 1       Select window 1
PFX 2       Select window 2
PFX 3       Select window 3
PFX 4       Select window 4
PFX 5       Select window 5
PFX 6       Select window 6
PFX 7       Select window 7
PFX 8       Select window 8
PFX 9       Select window 9
PFX :       Prompt for a command
PFX ;       Move to the previously active pane
PFX <       Display window menu
PFX =       Choose a paste buffer from a list
PFX >       Display pane menu
PFX ?       List key bindings
PFX @       Toggle pane between floating and tiled
PFX C       Customize options
PFX D       Choose and detach a client from a list
PFX E       Spread panes out evenly
PFX L       Switch to the last client
PFX M       Clear the marked pane
PFX T       Change the pane title
PFX [       Enter copy mode
PFX ]       Paste the most recent paste buffer
PFX c       Create a new window
PFX d       Detach the current client
PFX f       Search for a pane
PFX g       Move a floating pane
PFX i       Display window information
PFX l       Select the previously current window
PFX m       Toggle the marked pane
PFX n       Select the next window
PFX o       Select the next pane
PFX p       Select the previous window
PFX q       Display pane numbers
PFX r       Redraw the current client
PFX s       Choose a session from a list
PFX t       Show a clock
PFX w       Choose a window from a list
PFX x       Kill the active pane
PFX z       Zoom the active pane
PFX {       Swap the active pane with the pane above
PFX }       Swap the active pane with the pane below
PFX ~       Show messages
PFX DC      Reset so the visible part of the window follows the cursor
PFX PPage   Enter copy mode and scroll up
PFX BTab    Switch to a session
PFX Up      Select the pane above the active pane
PFX Down    Select the pane below the active pane
PFX Left    Select the pane to the left of the active pane
PFX Right   Select the pane to the right of the active pane
PFX M-1     Set the even-horizontal layout
PFX M-2     Set the even-vertical layout
PFX M-3     Set the main-horizontal layout
PFX M-4     Set the main-vertical layout
PFX M-5     Select the tiled layout
PFX M-6     Set the main-horizontal-mirrored layout
PFX M-7     Set the main-vertical-mirrored layout
PFX M-n     Select the next window with an alert
PFX M-o     Rotate through the panes in reverse
PFX M-p     Select the previous window with an alert
PFX M-Up    Resize the pane up by 5
PFX M-Down  Resize the pane down by 5
PFX M-Left  Resize the pane left by 5
PFX M-Right Resize the pane right by 5
PFX C-b     Send the prefix key
PFX C-o     Rotate through the panes
PFX C-z     Suspend the current client
PFX C-Up    Resize the pane up
PFX C-Down  Resize the pane down
PFX C-Left  Resize the pane left
PFX C-Right Resize the pane right
PFX S-Up    Move the visible part of the window up
PFX S-Down  Move the visible part of the window down
PFX S-Left  Move the visible part of the window left
PFX S-Right Move the visible part of the window right

bind-key  -T move ,       if-shell -F "#{pane_floating_flag}" { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x P -y P Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } }
bind-key  -T move .       if-shell -F "#{pane_floating_flag}" { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x P -y P Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } }
bind-key  -T move 0       resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" \; move-pane -P top-left

bind-key  -T root M-MouseDown3Pane          display-menu -T "#[align=centre]#{pane_index} (#{pane_id})" -t = -x M -y M "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},Go To Top,}" < { send-keys -X history-top } "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},Go To Bottom,}" > { send-keys -X history-bottom } '' "#{?#{==:#{pane_mode},copy-mode},#{?copy_line_numbers,Hide Line Numbers,Show Line Numbers},}" L { send-keys -X line-numbers-toggle } "#{?#{==:#{pane_mode},copy-mode},#{?refresh_active,Refresh Off,Refresh On},}" r { send-keys -X refresh-toggle } '' "#{?#{&&:#{buffer_size},#{!:#{pane_in_mode}}},Paste #[underscore]#{=/9/...:buffer_sample},}" p { paste-buffer } '' "#{?mouse_word,Search For #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-r { if-shell -F "#{?#{m/r:(copy|view)-mode,#{pane_mode}},0,1}" "copy-mode -t=" ; send-keys -X -t = search-backward -- "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" C-y { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_word,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_word},}" c { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_word}" } "#{?mouse_line,Copy Line,}" l { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_line}" } '' "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Type #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" C-h { copy-mode -q ; send-keys -l "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } "#{?mouse_hyperlink,Copy #[underscore]#{=/9/...:mouse_hyperlink},}" h { copy-mode -q ; set-buffer "#{q:mouse_hyperlink}" } '' "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move" -x L -y L Centre c { move-pane -P centre } '' "Top Left" 1 { move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Move & Resize,}" '' { display-menu -T "#[align=centre]Move & Resize" -x L -y L Fill 0 { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P top-left } '' "Top Left" 1 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-left } "Top Right" 2 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-right } "Bottom Left" 3 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-left } "Bottom Right" 4 { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-right } '' Top t { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P top-centre } Bottom b { resize-pane -x "100%" -y "50%" ; move-pane -P bottom-centre } Left l { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-left } Right r { resize-pane -x "50%" -y "100%" ; move-pane -P centre-right } } "#{?#{#{pane_floating_flag}},Tile,}" t { join-pane } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Float,}" f { break-pane -W } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Horizontal Split,}" h { split-window -h } "#{?#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},Vertical Split,}" v { split-window -v } '' "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Up,}" u { swap-pane -U } "#{?#{&&:#{!:#{pane_floating_flag}},#{>:#{window_panes},1}},Swap Down,}" d { swap-pane -D } "#{?pane_marked_set,,-}Swap Marked" s { swap-pane } '' Kill X { kill-pane } Respawn R { respawn-pane -k } "#{?pane_marked,Unmark,Mark}" m { select-pane -m } "#{?#{>:#{window_panes},1},,-}#{?window_zoomed_flag,Unzoom,Zoom}" z { resize-pane -Z }

table b doesn't exist

%%
### list-note-width
$ bind-Nthe note-TnnC-Spacedisplay
$ bind-Nn2-Tnnxdisplay
$ list-keys-N-Tnn
$ list-keys-Tnn
C-b x       n2
C-b C-Space the note
bind-key  -T nn x       display-message
bind-key  -T nn C-Space display-message
%%
### list-utf8-width
$ bind-Tuu日display
$ bind-Tuuxdisplay
$ bind-Tuuédisplay
$ list-keys-Tuu
$ list-keys-N-a-Tuu
bind-key  -T uu x  display-message
bind-key  -T uu é  display-message
bind-key  -T uu 日 display-message
C-b x  display-message
C-b é  display-message
C-b 日 display-message
%%
### list-bind-escape
$ bind-Tee\;display
$ bind-Tee"display
$ bind-Tee'display
$ bind-Tee#display
$ bind-Tee$display
$ bind-Tee~display
$ bind-Tee%display
$ bind-Tee{display
$ bind-Tee}display
$ bind-Tee\display
$ bind-Tee display
$ bind-Tee0x7fdisplay
$ bind-Tee0x1display
$ list-keys-Tee
bind-key  -T ee [SOH] display-message
bind-key  -T ee Space display-message
bind-key  -T ee \"    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \#    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \$    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \%    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \'    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \;    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \\    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \{    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \}    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \~    display-message
bind-key  -T ee \177  display-message

### bind-new
$ bind-Tzzxdisplay
$ list-keys-Tzz

unknown key: x

### bind-rebind

$ bind-Tzzxdisplayb
$ list-keys-Tzz

C-b x my note

### bind-argv-semicolons
$ bind-Tzzxdisplaya;displayb
$ bind-Tzzydisplaya;
$ bind-Tzzzdisplaya\;
$ list-keys-Tzz
bind-key  -T zz x display-message a
bind-key  -T zz y display-message a
bind-key  -T zz z display-message a
%%
### bind-argv-string
$ bind-Tzzxdisplay a ; display b
$ bind-Tzzydisplay -p 'a b'
$ bind-Tzzzno-such-command
$ bind-Tzzwdisplay -Z
$ list-keys-Tzz
unknown command: no-such-command
command display-message: unknown flag -Z
bind-key  -T zz x display-message a \; display-message b
bind-key  -T zz y display-message -p "a b"
%%
### bind-argv-string-env
$ bind-Tzzxdisplay $HOME ~
$ list-keys-Tzz
command display-message: too many arguments (need at most 1)
table zz doesn't exist
%%
### bind-argv-multi
$ bind-Tzzxdisplay-pa bc
$ bind-Tzzydisplay-Z
$ bind-Tzzznosuchx
$ list-keys-Tzz
command display-message: too many arguments (need at most 1)
command display-message: unknown flag -Z
unknown command: nosuch
table zz doesn't exist
%%
### bind-argv-alias
$ bind-Tzzxsplitp-h
$ bind-Tzzysend-Xcancel
$ bind-Tzzzinfo
$ list-keys-Tzz
bind-key  -T zz x split-window -h
bind-key  -T zz y send-keys -X cancel
bind-key  -T zz z show-messages -JT
%%
### bind-unknown-key
$ bindnosuchkeydisplay
unknown key: nosuchkey
%%
### bind-none
$ bindNonedisplay
unknown key: None
%%
### bind-f13
$ bindF13display
unknown key: F13
%%
### bind-missing
$ bind
command bind-key: too few arguments (need at least 1)
%%
### bind-any
$ bind-TzzAnydisplay
$ list-keys-Tzz
bind-key  -T zz Any display-message
%%
### bind-flags
$ bind-Xxdisplay
command bind-key: unknown flag -X
%%
### bind-N-needs-arg
$ bind-N
command bind-key: -N expects an argument
%%
### bind-T-needs-arg
$ bind-T
command bind-key: -T expects an argument
%%
### unbind-missing
$ unbind
missing key
%%
### unbind-missing-q
$ unbind-q
%%
### unbind-key-a
$ unbind-ax
key given with -a
%%
### unbind-key-a-q
$ unbind-aqx
%%
### unbind-a-nonexistent
$ unbind-a-Tnope
table nope doesn't exist
%%
### unbind-a-nonexistent-q
$ unbind-aq-Tnope
%%
### unbind-a-prefix
$ unbind-a
$ list-keys-Tprefix
$ list-keys-Troot-1
table prefix doesn't exist
bind-key  -T root MouseDown1Pane select-pane -t = \; send-keys -M
%%
### unbind-a-n
$ unbind-an
$ list-keys-Troot
$ list-keys-TprefixC-b
bind-key  -T prefix C-b send-prefix
%%
### unbind-a-T
$ unbind-a-Tcopy-mode
$ list-keys-Tcopy-mode
table copy-mode doesn't exist
%%
### unbind-T-nonexistent
$ unbind-Tnopex
table nope doesn't exist
%%
### unbind-T-nonexistent-q
$ unbind-qTnopex
%%
### unbind-none
$ unbindNone
unknown key: None
%%
### unbind-none-q
$ unbind-qNone
%%
### unbind-unknown
$ unbindnosuchkey
unknown key: nosuchkey
%%
### unbind-unbound
$ unbindx
$ list-keys-Tprefixx
unknown key: x
%%
### unbind-default
$ unbindC-b
$ list-keys-N-TprefixC-b
$ list-keys-Tprefix-1
unknown key: C-b
bind-key  -T prefix Tab new-pane -E -X 0 -Y 0 -x "75%" -y "30%" \; move-pane -P bottom-centre \; switch-mode -kw
%%
### unbind-n
$ unbind-nMouseDown1Pane
$ list-keys-TrootMouseDown1Pane
unknown key: MouseDown1Pane
%%
### unbind-last-removes-table
$ bind-Tzzxdisplay
$ unbind-Tzzx
$ list-keys-Tzz
table zz doesn't exist
%%
### unbind-default-table-stays
$ unbind-Tmove1
$ unbind-Tmove2
$ list-keys-Tmove-N
C-b ,       Display move menu
C-b .       Display move and resize menu
C-b 0       Move pane to fill the window
C-b 3       Move pane to bottom-left corner
C-b 4       Move pane to bottom-right corner
C-b Up      Move pane to top
C-b Down    Move pane to bottom
C-b Left    Move pane to left
C-b Right   Move pane to right
C-b M-1     Move pane to top-left corner and resize
C-b M-2     Move pane to top-right corner and resize
C-b M-3     Move pane to bottom-left corner and resize
C-b M-4     Move pane to bottom-right corner and resize
C-b M-Up    Move pane to top and resize
C-b M-Down  Move pane to bottom and resize
C-b M-Left  Move pane to left and resize
C-b M-Right Move pane to right and resize
%%
### unbind-table-then-listing
$ unbind-a-Tmove
$ list-keys-Tmove
$ list-keys-N
table move doesn't exist
C-b Tab     Switch to a window
